# Owner decisions

Every decision the owner took on WebKit 6.0, one row each, so none slips. The rule that keeps it current is in [`.claude/rules/git-workflow.md`](../rules/git-workflow.md): a change that records or implements an owner decision adds or updates its row here in the same change.

Status is one of **done** (in the code or the docs), **open** (decided, not finished), **deferred** (decided to do later; the roadmap holds it) or **needs owner** (waits on an answer or an action only the owner can give). "Where" names the commit or file that holds it. Deferred and open items are also listed in [`ROADMAP.md`](../../ROADMAP.md) (M2.13, "Deferred and open").

This register starts from the review of 12–16 November 2026 (192 rows: 178 done, 3 open, 7 deferred, 4 needs owner; updated 2026-11-16 with the owner's answers on APCA, the Eyebrow orange, code owners, 1Password and the round-2 site questions).

(The Edge Orchestrator part is superseded by IC12, 2026-11-18: the product is now Orchestrator.)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | done   | `icons.json` entries `edge-orchestrator`, `edge-nodes`, `edge-services`, `edge-libraries`, `durable-objects`                                                                   |

The products are the ones WebKit shows (console mock-up plates, product diagrams) and the `azion-kb` product list. Five were missing and are drawn new, in the 1.2 px outline style: `cache` (Cache), `network-shield` (Network Shield), `ddos-protection` (DDoS Protection), `secure-internet-access` (Secure Internet Access), and `tiered-cache` as a Cache feature with its own drawing (undoes the IC06 merge into `layers`). Not added: Events (pre-launch in `azion-kb`; Real-Time Events stays the product), Our Network (platform page, not a product). `test/catalog/product-icons.test.mjs` fails the build when a plate product or a product diagram has no icon. | done   | `packages/icons/src/svg-raw/az` (5 new SVGs), `icons.json`, `v6-icons.json`, diagrams `cache`, `network-shield`, `ddos-protection`, examples that showed Cache or Tiered Cache |

## 2026-11-17 — Navigation levels and drawer width

| ID   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Status |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ |
| NV01 | Page navigation lives in the rail, two levels at most: a product with 3 or fewer resources lists them on level 1, a product with more is a drill row whose level 2 lists them. A page has only its `PageTabs` (at most 7; more pages become sections of one tab's page). No menu or other navigation inside a page. | done   |
| NV02 | The in-page local navigation is gone: the `detail/sectioned-resource` block (never released) is removed, and `webkit/no-page-navigation-menu` has no block exemption and no `webkit-local-navigation` marker.                                                                                                       | done   |
| NV03 | The large drawer, the default and the widest, is 40% of the screen (at least `--container-lg`); medium is 30% (at least `--container-md`); small stays `--container-sm`.                                                                                                                                            | done   |

## 2026-11-18 — Error pages: edge and console families (lane R)

| RV-R1 | RV-D1 decided by the owner: error pages are two block families, never a component (Cloudscape, Atlassian, Polaris and Primer treat error pages as page patterns; Cloudflare, Fastly and Vercel serve theirs as hosted templates). (1) `state/state-edge-error-page`, surface `edge`: what visitors of Azion customers' sites see when the edge cannot serve them, in Option 3's diagnostic format — the request path You → Azion edge → Origin with every hop the same width and height, What happened? / What can I do?, request details — and never an account action (no sign in, switch account or request access); states 502 (primary), 504, 500, 503, 403 (the site's firewall), 429, 404. (2) `state/state-error-page`, surface `app`: Azion's own console, site and docs, in Option 1's quiet style with the existing `StatusCode` as the figure and a code, request ID and time line; states 404 (primary), 401, 403, 429, 500, 503 and a missing record. The console page shows the `StatusCode` instead of RV04's 404 numeral art. | done | `packages/webkit/blocks/state/state-edge-error-page/`, `packages/webkit/blocks/state/state-error-page/`, `src/test/blocks/state/state-edge-error-page.test.ts`, `src/test/blocks/state/state-error-page.test.ts`; the proposal page (`docs/reviews/error-page-alternatives.html`, `/proposal/error-pages.html`) opens with the decision |
| RV-R2 | `FlowDiagram` gets a `uniform` prop: every step takes the width and height of the largest one, so a request path whose steps carry different text reads as identical boxes (the owner's screenshot of Option 3 showed a taller middle node). Reused rather than a new component: FlowDiagram already draws the nodes, ports and connectors. | done | `.specs/flow-diagram.md` (v8), `src/components/data/flow-diagram/flow-diagram.vue`, `examples/flow-diagram/uniform.vue`, `docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md` (Budget-raise flow-diagram-root) |
| RV-R3 | A fourth block surface, `edge`: pages Azion's edge serves to the visitors of a customer's site, with no Azion product chrome and no account actions; the MCP `surface` filter takes it. Kept apart from `app` (console), `site` (azion.com) and `docs` so an agent building a console page never picks the edge page, and the reverse. | done | `packages/webkit/scripts/lib/surfaces.mjs`, `packages/webkit-mcp/src/server.js`, `catalog.json#surfaces` |
| RV-U1 | A Stats figure never wraps (owner 2026-11-18: "the break on stats cannot happen, so always resize to fit the screen"). `99.99 %` and `4.5 TBPS` used to drop the unit to a second line in narrow columns. Each cell is now a size container, the figure is `nowrap`, and the `text-big-number-*` roles set it at the smaller of their step and `(100cqi − gap width) / (glyphs × 0.65)` (Proto Mono glyphs measure 0.64–0.65 em in Chromium). Stats writes the glyph count of its longest figure into `--text-big-number-characters` and the width of the gaps between prefix, value and suffix into `--text-big-number-inset`, so every figure in a band is set at the same size, on the same line, with no JS measuring. Cells align to the top, so a two-line caption no longer moves its figure. | done | `packages/theme/src/tokens/semantic/typography.js` (`fontSizeFit`), `scripts/build-tokens.mjs`, `token-export.js`, `token-purpose.js`; `stats.vue`, `stats.test.ts` (styled fit test, 320–1600 px, 2–4 columns, every size); `.specs/stats.md`, `.specs/stats-section.md`; budgets in `docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md` |

## 2026-11-18 — Floating overlays closed on load (lane X)

| ID    | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Status                                                    | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RV-X1 | A floating overlay (popover, tooltip, dropdown menu, context menu, hover card, select / multi-select / combobox list, date picker panel, command palette, dialog, drawer) never starts open on page load in an example or a block's primary state; it opens on the reader's click, hover or focus (owner 2026-11-18: "some popovers come onto the screen; they should only appear if I click", same complaint as the Hotspot/TutorialPanel fix). Inline disclosures (accordion, sidebar groups, Reasoning, Sources, ToolCall details, tree nodes) may start open. A block variant that exists to show an overlay state keeps it open: the reader picks it from the state switcher and it renders in its own frame. The eleven offending examples (Popover and Tooltip AutoPlacement, six DropdownMenu examples, ContextMenu WithSubMenu, HoverCard ResourcePreview, MultiSelect ListStates) now open from their trigger, with summaries that say how. Proposed line for COMPONENT_REQUIREMENTS.md's examples section, pending owner approval: "A floating overlay in an example starts closed and opens on the reader's action (rules/webkit-overlays-closed-on-load.md)."                                                                                                                                              | done — COMPONENT_REQUIREMENTS line pending owner approval | `rules/webkit-overlays-closed-on-load.md`; `adapters/AGENTS_FRAGMENT.md`; `packages/webkit/test/standards/overlays-closed-on-load.test.mjs`; `packages/webkit/src/test/overlays-closed-on-load.test.ts`; `examples/{popover,tooltip,dropdown-menu,context-menu,hover-card,multi-select}/`; `.specs/{dropdown-menu,context-menu,hover-card}.md` |

A naked root draws the connectors; `nodes` + `edges` render the richest card by default (icon and type, linked title, description, status dot and text, metric items, details that open, labelled and coloured edges, a hidden list of connections), and the parts (`Node`, `Group`, `Anchor`, `Header`, `Title`, `Description`, `Footer`, `Item`) compose by hand. Accepted as recommended: the bind control is the `FlowDiagramBind` companion (`./flow-diagram-bind`, not attached), status is a dot and text, and `orientation="responsive"` (the default) stacks into a rail on phones. A title row with controls keeps `--spacing-sm` between its buttons and the divider below. 6.0 clean break: `TopologyDiagram` and `FlowDiagram.Parallel` are removed and mapped in `v6.json` (`topology-diagram` → `flow-diagram`, `flow-diagram-parallel` → `flow-diagram-group`); `levels` → `nodes` + `edges` and the bind events are a manual step in MIGRATION_V6.md. The edge error page (RV-Z2) now draws its path with FlowDiagram data cards. | done                                                      | `.specs/flow-diagram.md`; `packages/webkit/src/components/data/flow-diagram/`; `examples/flow-diagram/`; `src/cli/migrations/v6*.json`; `MIGRATION_V6.md`; proposal page `packages/webkit/docs/reviews/diagram-proposal.html` (`/proposal/diagram.html`, with a Decision note)                                                                 |

| RV-D2b | The Relative tab lists the quick ranges and one Custom range row; the Duration and Unit form opens only when that row is chosen, in place of the list, with Back to quick ranges to return (focus moves in and back). Benchmarked: CloudWatch (quick picks with a Custom button that reveals duration and unit), Grafana and Datadog (quick list first, custom input secondary). | done | `.specs/date-range-picker.md` (v13); `date-range-picker.vue`; locale key `dateRangePicker.showQuickRanges` |
| RV-D2c | DateRangePicker limits: `min` defaults to the start of the day five years ago and `max` to the end of today (in the picker's zone, from the reference clock); a new `maxDuration` (`{ amount, unit }`, unset by default) caps the range length. The limits disable calendar days, months and years, hide quick ranges that do not fit, narrow the calendar while the end is picked, and refuse typed ranges and custom durations with a message (`Choose a range of 90 days or less.`). A later `max` allows future dates (maintenance window, alert schedule). DatePicker stays unbounded by default, because its single date is often in the future (token expiry); its spec says to set `min` to today there. New examples Limits and FutureDates. | done | `.specs/date-range-picker.md` (v13), `.specs/date-picker.md` (v14); `date-range-picker.vue`, `date-range.ts`; `examples/date-range-picker/{limits,future-dates}.vue` |

## 2026-11-18 — Filled list rows keep a gap (lane N)

| RV-N1 | Two filled rows of a list never touch (owner 2026-11-18: in an open Select the selected "System" row and the highlighted "Light" row merged into one block; "allow a small separation between the lines, ideally by reducing the height of the highlighted line"). Every popup option list and navigation list (Select, MultiSelect, Combobox, DropdownMenu and the ContextMenu built on it, Command, PropertyFilter suggestions, the PromptInput menu, SidebarMenu, Tree) keeps its rows flush and its row height (so the hit area and touch target do not change) and insets the fill 1px from the top and bottom edge with `border-y border-transparent bg-clip-padding forced-colors:border-y-0`, leaving a 2px gap between two fills, as Radix and shadcn menus, Linear, Vercel Geist and Raycast do. Combobox and PropertyFilter drop their 4px row gap for the same look as Select. Panel lists that already keep a 4px gap and draw their focus ring outside the row (TransferList, ConversationHistory, FilterMenu, the DateRangePicker presets) keep it. `forced-colors:border-y-0` is needed because Windows high contrast paints a transparent border as a solid line (Tree had this bug too). DropdownMenu rows (and so ContextMenu) now draw their focus ring inset like Select, so the ring no longer runs into the neighbouring fill. | done | `.claude/rules/styling.md` and `rules/webkit-styling.md` § "Two filled rows never touch"; `packages/webkit/src/test/list-row-gap.test.ts`; specs of select, multi-select, combobox, dropdown-menu, command, property-filter, prompt-input, sidebar-menu, tree; budgets in `docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md` |

Today's uses stay: behind band text, and under the opaque windows the bands were designed around (console mock-up, terminal, code file). `ConsoleShowcaseSection` paints its texture only for `variant: 'console'`. `diagram` draws the scene on the plain canvas and keeps its bottom padding at every width. ## 2026-11-18 — The hero keeps its dots behind the copy, never under a diagram (lane H2)

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Status                           | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RV-H2-1 | Follows RV-T2-1 in the hero. `Hero` keeps its dot backdrop behind the copy, but never under a diagram, chart, stat or page-JSON content scene (`data-plain-ground` outside a `data-opaque` window): with one in the `bottom` window the backdrop stops at that window's top edge, with one in the `top` window or the `media` cell the band paints no backdrop. A console window, illustration, globe or command keeps the dots under it. `HeroSection` inherits it, so a `center` hero with a topology or a page-JSON `FlowDiagram` scene shows the dots behind the headline and a plain ground under the diagram. The `texture-backdrop` check models the hero's windows: a graphic in the copy column or the `background` slot of a textured `Hero`, or in the `bottom` window under a `floorTexture`, still fails; a graphic in the `top`, `bottom` or `media` window passes, because the component keeps the dots off it. | done (decided 2026-11-18, owner) | `hero.vue`; `.specs/hero.md` v9 and `.specs/hero-section.md` v14 (States); `src/eslint-plugin/lib/texture-backdrop.js`; `src/test/texture-backdrop.test.ts`; `apps/visual/tests/texture-backdrop.spec.ts`; `.claude/rules/styling.md` and `rules/webkit-styling.md` |

| AI-05   | The hand-written copy of TanStack AI's message types (AI-01's "each block declares the shape itself") is replaced by TanStack's own types (owner 2026-11-21): `@aziontech/webkit/utils/ai-chat` imports them type-only from `@tanstack/ai` and names each one `AiChat*` (`AiChatMessage` is `UIMessage`, `AiChatPart` is `MessagePart`, the parts, `ToolCallState`, `ContentPartSource`, `BatchInterruptError`), so the interface follows TanStack by bumping the version. `@tanstack/ai` is an optional peer `^1.5.0` and a dev dependency pinned to `1.5.0`; no runtime import, no markup, no styles. Kept on top: the typed `metadata` keys (sources, thinking time, stopped), `stop` and `input` on a sub-agent card (what `useChat` adds), and the types `@tanstack/ai` does not have (`AiChatTool`, interrupts, queue, connection, segments). Nothing visual changes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | done                                | `packages/webkit/src/utils/ai-chat.ts`, `packages/webkit/package.json`, `.claude/rules/dependencies.md` (granted exception), the three AI blocks' guidelines                                                                                                                               |
| AI-A    | `ToolCall` gains `streaming` (arguments still arriving, `Preparing…`, neutral spinner) and `canceled` (stopped before it finished, TanStack `outcome: 'cancelled'`), both shared status values; streaming is kept apart from `running` as AI Elements does, so a call that has not started never reads as running. `ToolApproval` gains opt-in `editable` + `invalid` and a default `v-model` (the arguments as text) for approving with edited arguments (TanStack `resolveInterrupt(true, { editedArgs })`); `approve` now emits `(event, input)` (a trailing argument, so no codemod); after a decision the field stays read-only as the record. | AI-B    | `Conversation` loads older messages (`moreMessages`, `loadingMore`, `load-more`, the data-table names): a Load earlier messages button at the top plus loading near the top, scroll anchoring so prepended messages move nothing in view, and no announcement of prepended messages. A reconnecting or connection-lost notice stays out of the components: the block shows an `Alert` or `StatusIndicator` above the prompt input from `connectionStatus` and `resuming`. | AI-C    | Queued messages are a new monolithic component, `prompt-queue`, not a PromptInput part: it sits above the composer box, not in it, and a part would need a new domain part word. It takes plain `items` (`id`, `text`, `attachmentCount`) a block fills from `useChat` `queue`, emits `remove (event, item)` for `cancelQueued(item.id)`, stays mounted with a polite status region, and keeps focus on the next remove button.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | done                                | `.specs/prompt-queue.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/prompt-queue/`, `packages/webkit/examples/prompt-queue/`, `promptQueue` locale keys                                                                                                                                           |
| AI-D    | A new `AgentQuestion` component (`ai/agent-question`) renders TanStack AI's generic interrupt, the agent asking the reader something in the middle of a run: `type` picks the control (`choice` radios, a one-line `text` field, a Yes/No `confirm`), Submit and an optional Skip, the shared `status` set (`running` while the answer is sent, `error` with its line, `success` keeping the answer as text, `skipped`, `canceled`). Library-neutral: a block maps `message`, the payload's options, `status` and `errors` onto props and calls `resolveInterrupt` / `cancel` from `submit` / `skip`. Built from the approved spec; budget 16 KB (15.37 KB measured); the `llms-full.txt` budget rises from 2,200,000 to 2,215,000 bytes because the new page did not fit the 3.4 KB left.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | done                                | `.specs/agent-question.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/agent-question/`, `packages/webkit/examples/agent-question/`, `packages/webkit/src/locales/*.ts`, `packages/webkit/scripts/build-llms.mjs`                                                                                  |
| AI-E    | `AgentActivity` (`@aziontech/webkit/agent-activity`, `ai`, monolithic) shows the steps an agent works through: the current step with its status, the full list behind a `{count} steps` disclosure, one polite announcement per step that stays current for a second and one per outcome (finished, stopped, failed). Plain props (`steps`: `key`, `title`, shared `status`, `description`), so a block fills it from TanStack AI `activity` parts with no import. The `llms-full.txt` cap moves from 2,200,000 to 2,210,000 bytes: the base had 3.4 KB of headroom and the page adds 7.7 KB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | done                                | `.specs/agent-activity.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/agent-activity/`, `packages/webkit/examples/agent-activity/`, `agentActivity` in `src/locales/{en,es,pt-BR}.ts`, `scripts/build-llms.mjs`                                                                                   |
| AI-F    | New `SubagentCard` (`ai/subagent-card`) for TanStack AI `subagent` parts: it names the helper agent, shows its status in the shared status set (the block maps `finished` to `success`, `suspended` to `pending`, a stopped `error` to `canceled`), its elapsed time, a summary line and `Stop` while it runs, and opens to a default slot where the block renders the child's messages; the card renders no messages and imports nothing from TanStack. A new component rather than new props on `ToolCall`, which records one tool call.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | done                                | `.specs/subagent-card.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/subagent-card/`, `packages/webkit/examples/subagent-card/`, `src/locales/*` (`subagentCard`)                                                                                                                                 |
| AI-G    | New component `AudioPlayer` (`@aziontech/webkit/audio-player`, content): plays one clip on the native audio element with play/pause, a seek bar between the current time and the duration, mute and volume, an optional speed control (`playbackRates`), an optional Download link (`downloadable`, `fileName`) and a transcript disclosure; it owns the loading (generating), buffering and error states. Library-neutral: a block fills `src` from a TanStack `audio` part (a URL as is, base64 as a data URL) or a `useGenerateSpeech` / `useGenerateAudio` result, and `duration` from the result.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | done                                | `.specs/audio-player.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/audio-player/`, `packages/webkit/examples/audio-player/`, `audioPlayer` in the locales                                                                                                                                   |
| AI-H    | A new `content/video-player` plays TanStack AI `video` parts and `useGenerateVideo` results: a native video element with play and pause, seek with time, mute and volume, captions and full screen, no autoplay and no player library. Its `status` (`pending`, `running`, `success`, `error`), `progress` and `error` props take the generation job's state, so the generating and failed states render in place of the video. Reuse audit: `embed`, `doc-frame`, `file-preview` and the marketing media play no file with controls.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | done                                | `.specs/video-player.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/video-player/`, `packages/webkit/examples/video-player/`, `videoPlayer` locale keys                                                                                                                                      |
| AI-I    | Images in a response are `ImageGallery` (`@aziontech/webkit/image-gallery`, content): a grid of thumbnails from a URL or base64 data and its media type, a `Dialog` viewer with Previous, Next and Download, a `Skeleton` per expected image while `loading`, and the `error` state. A block fills `items` from TanStack AI `image` parts and `useGenerateImage` results; the component imports nothing from TanStack.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | done                                | `.specs/image-gallery.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/image-gallery/`, `packages/webkit/examples/image-gallery/`                                                                                                                                                              |
| AI-J    | New component `mcp-app-frame` (category `ai`) renders the MCP app widget a TanStack `ui-resource` part carries: a sandboxed `iframe` (`allow-scripts` only, opaque origin, `srcdoc` with an injected content policy, a URL only through the app's `resolveUrl` and only `https:`), loading and blocked states, auto-height from validated size messages, a `message` event that forwards validated widget messages to the app and an exposed `post()` for replies; it never acts on a message. Named `mcp-app-frame`, not `app-frame`, because `app-*` is the console shell; `embed` stays the trusted, author-placed frame. Threat model in the spec. Budgets raised for the new pages: `llms-full.txt` 2,200,000 → 2,215,000 bytes, unpacked package 33,400,000 → 33,420,000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | done                                | `.specs/mcp-app-frame.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/mcp-app-frame/`, `packages/webkit/examples/mcp-app-frame/`, `src/locales/*`                                                                                                                                                  |
| AI-K    | `FilePreview` (spec v2) previews a PDF in the browser's own viewer (an `object` of the source, `var(--container-lg)` tall, named `Preview of <file>`, falling back to the no-preview body where the browser has no PDF viewer), recognizes audio and video files as a card with Download (no player), and takes `base64` bytes (plain or a data URL) so a TanStack `document` or `image` part (`source.type: 'data'`, `value`, `mimeType`) maps without an adapter; it makes and revokes its own object URL and decodes text as UTF-8. | AI-M    | Bring your own key is a block, `ai/ai-api-key`: pick a provider, paste the key in a masked field (never prefilled, cleared once saved), see only its masked tail, Test connection (pending, running, success, error) and Remove key after a confirm. It stores nothing and emits `save`, `test` and `remove`, so an app wires it to TanStack AI's `useByok` (`update`, `clear`, the snapshot's masked `status`) or its own store; no new component and no dependency. The `llms-full.txt` budget (2,206,000) and the unpacked package budget (33,410,000) were raised by the size of the new block.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | done                                | `packages/webkit/blocks/ai/ai-api-key/`, `src/test/blocks/ai/ai-api-key.test.ts`, `scripts/build-llms.mjs`, `scripts/package-size-budget.json`                                                                                                                                             |
| AI-INT  | The AI blocks draw every new piece from TanStack AI's own parts, with no conversion in the app: `image` parts in a row become one `ImageGallery`, `audio` an `AudioPlayer`, `video` a `VideoPlayer`, `document` a `FilePreview` (named by `metadata.name`), `subagent` a `SubagentCard` (`finished` success, `suspended` pending, an `error` of `Stopped` canceled, Stop calls `stop()`), and `activity` parts plus the `activity` messages before a turn one `AgentActivity` that reads `content.title`, `content.status` and `content.detail`. A pending generic interrupt is an `AgentQuestion` after the turn it arrived in (Submit calls `resolveInterrupt(answer)`, Skip `cancel()`), kept with its outcome once it leaves `interrupts`. A tool with `editable` lets the reader edit its JSON arguments and resolves `{ editedArgs }`; `input-streaming` reads `streaming` and a `cancelled` result `canceled`. The blocks take `queue` (shown in a `PromptQueue`; the composer then sends while busy through the new opt-in PromptInput `queueable`, spec v7), `moreMessages` / `loadingMore` / `load-more` for `Conversation`, and `connection` / `resuming` for a Reconnecting status or a Connection lost alert. Fixes: ToolCall and inline code use `text-label-code-sm`; the audio and video seek bars stay left to right in right-to-left (specs v2). `ui-resource` stays skipped until the MCP app frame lands. | done                                | `packages/webkit/blocks/ai/*`, `.specs/prompt-input.md`, `src/components/ai/prompt-input/`, `src/components/ai/tool-call/`, `src/components/content/{audio,video}-player/`, `apps/demos/src/pages/ai-chat-page.vue`                                                                        |
| AI-SIZE | The size limits move once for the whole AI run, from the merged tree: `llms-full.txt` 2,200,000 → 2,300,000 bytes (measured 2,292,628 after merging LanguageSelect), `catalog.json` 1.36 → 1.385 MB (measured 1,375,768), `catalog/` 1.34 → 1.375 MB (1,364,289), the package unpacked 33.4 → 34.5 MB (33,991,246) and packed 8.05 → 8.26 MB (8,132,209). The growth is text only: eight new components and one block, each with its source, examples, catalog and llms pages; the lanes' separate raises are replaced by these.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | done                                | `packages/webkit/scripts/build-llms.mjs`, `scripts/package-size-budget.json`, `test/catalog/catalog-split.test.mjs`                                                                                                                                                                        |
| AI-GAP  | The chat blocks close the last TanStack AI gaps with no adapter. `attachable` (with `accept`, `maxFiles`, `maxFileSize`) adds `PromptInputAttachButton`, and `send` then hands over TanStack's `MultimodalContent` (`{ content: [text, image/audio/video/document parts with base64 data sources] }`; still a plain string without files), so `@send="(_e, content) => sendMessage(content)"`; user turns draw their media parts. `structured-output` parts draw as a JSON `CodeBlock` (`partial` or `raw` while streaming, `data` when complete, an `Alert` on error). A `tool-result` with text and image content parts draws as text and an `ImageGallery`. `interruptErrors` shows an `Alert` with Retry that emits `retry-interrupts` (`retryInterrupts()`); `unbound` interrupts are asked as an `AgentQuestion` without Skip and emit `answer-interrupt (event, interrupt, value)` for the app to route, since the chat cannot resolve them. `hydrating` (`isHydrating`) shows `Skeleton` rows and disables the composer; `sessionGenerating` shows Responding in another tab. A tool marked `answerable` (with `options`) in `tools` is asked as an `AgentQuestion` until it has an output, and emits `answer-tool (event, { toolCallId, tool, output })` for `addToolResult`. `ai-action-approval` takes the structured output, rich results, interrupt errors and generic or unbound questions, not attachments.    | done                                | `packages/webkit/blocks/ai/{ai-chat,ai-chat-drawer,ai-action-approval}`, `src/test/blocks/ai/ai-chat.test.ts`, `src/test/blocks/ai.test.ts`, `apps/demos/src/pages/ai-chat-page.vue`                                                                                                       |
| PH-SIZE | Every action in a `PageHeader` is `size="medium"` (owner 2026-11-19: Create Resource was 40 px beside the 32 px Documentation on `/resources`). The create action stands out by colour, `variant="primary"`; Documentation and the others are `variant="outline"`. This reverses the "page's only large control" ladder of `skills/webkit-lists` (M1.10 row 29) and the "both outlined" line of the PageHeader spec, which disagreed. Nine screens fixed; `webkit/consistent-header-action-size` (ESLint, error in both presets) fails any other size.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | done                                | `.specs/page-header.md` (v8), `skills/webkit-lists`, `skills/webkit-navigation`, `rules/webkit-page-layout.md`, `consistent-header-action-size.js`, blocks `view-table`, `view-card`, `edit-variables`, `account-*` (5), `apps/demos/src/blocks/table-view.vue`                            |

| AI-F    | New `SubagentCard` (`ai/subagent-card`) for TanStack AI `subagent` parts: it names the helper agent, shows its status in the shared status set (the block maps `finished` to `success`, `suspended` to `pending`, a stopped `error` to `canceled`), its elapsed time, a summary line and `Stop` while it runs, and opens to a default slot where the block renders the child's messages; the card renders no messages and imports nothing from TanStack. A new component rather than new props on `ToolCall`, which records one tool call.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | done                                | `.specs/subagent-card.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/subagent-card/`, `packages/webkit/examples/subagent-card/`, `src/locales/*` (`subagentCard`)                                                                                                                                 |
| AI-G    | New component `AudioPlayer` (`@aziontech/webkit/audio-player`, content): plays one clip on the native audio element with play/pause, a seek bar between the current time and the duration, mute and volume, an optional speed control (`playbackRates`), an optional Download link (`downloadable`, `fileName`) and a transcript disclosure; it owns the loading (generating), buffering and error states. Library-neutral: a block fills `src` from a TanStack `audio` part (a URL as is, base64 as a data URL) or a `useGenerateSpeech` / `useGenerateAudio` result, and `duration` from the result.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | done                                | `.specs/audio-player.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/audio-player/`, `packages/webkit/examples/audio-player/`, `audioPlayer` in the locales                                                                                                                                   |
| AI-H    | A new `content/video-player` plays TanStack AI `video` parts and `useGenerateVideo` results: a native video element with play and pause, seek with time, mute and volume, captions and full screen, no autoplay and no player library. Its `status` (`pending`, `running`, `success`, `error`), `progress` and `error` props take the generation job's state, so the generating and failed states render in place of the video. Reuse audit: `embed`, `doc-frame`, `file-preview` and the marketing media play no file with controls.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | done                                | `.specs/video-player.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/video-player/`, `packages/webkit/examples/video-player/`, `videoPlayer` locale keys                                                                                                                                      |
| AI-I    | Images in a response are `ImageGallery` (`@aziontech/webkit/image-gallery`, content): a grid of thumbnails from a URL or base64 data and its media type, a `Dialog` viewer with Previous, Next and Download, a `Skeleton` per expected image while `loading`, and the `error` state. A block fills `items` from TanStack AI `image` parts and `useGenerateImage` results; the component imports nothing from TanStack.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | done                                | `.specs/image-gallery.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/image-gallery/`, `packages/webkit/examples/image-gallery/`                                                                                                                                                              |
| AI-J    | New component `mcp-app-frame` (category `ai`) renders the MCP app widget a TanStack `ui-resource` part carries: a sandboxed `iframe` (`allow-scripts` only, opaque origin, `srcdoc` with an injected content policy, a URL only through the app's `resolveUrl` and only `https:`), loading and blocked states, auto-height from validated size messages, a `message` event that forwards validated widget messages to the app and an exposed `post()` for replies; it never acts on a message. Named `mcp-app-frame`, not `app-frame`, because `app-*` is the console shell; `embed` stays the trusted, author-placed frame. Threat model in the spec. Budgets raised for the new pages: `llms-full.txt` 2,200,000 → 2,215,000 bytes, unpacked package 33,400,000 → 33,420,000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | done                                | `.specs/mcp-app-frame.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/ai/mcp-app-frame/`, `packages/webkit/examples/mcp-app-frame/`, `src/locales/*`                                                                                                                                                  |
| AI-K    | `FilePreview` (spec v2) previews a PDF in the browser's own viewer (an `object` of the source, `var(--container-lg)` tall, named `Preview of <file>`, falling back to the no-preview body where the browser has no PDF viewer), recognizes audio and video files as a card with Download (no player), and takes `base64` bytes (plain or a data URL) so a TanStack `document` or `image` part (`source.type: 'data'`, `value`, `mimeType`) maps without an adapter; it makes and revokes its own object URL and decodes text as UTF-8. Budget 6.9 → 7.6 KB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | done                                | `.specs/file-preview.md`, `packages/webkit/src/components/content/file-preview/`, `packages/webkit/examples/file-preview/pdf.vue`, `src/locales/*.ts` (`filePreview.document`)                                                                                                             |
| AI-M    | Bring your own key is a block, `ai/ai-api-key`: pick a provider, paste the key in a masked field (never prefilled, cleared once saved), see only its masked tail, Test connection (pending, running, success, error) and Remove key after a confirm. It stores nothing and emits `save`, `test` and `remove`, so an app wires it to TanStack AI's `useByok` (`update`, `clear`, the snapshot's masked `status`) or its own store; no new component and no dependency. The `llms-full.txt` budget (2,206,000) and the unpacked package budget (33,410,000) were raised by the size of the new block.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | done                                | `packages/webkit/blocks/ai/ai-api-key/`, `src/test/blocks/ai/ai-api-key.test.ts`, `scripts/build-llms.mjs`, `scripts/package-size-budget.json`                                                                                                                                             |
| AI-INT  | The AI blocks draw every new piece from TanStack AI's own parts, with no conversion in the app: `image` parts in a row become one `ImageGallery`, `audio` an `AudioPlayer`, `video` a `VideoPlayer`, `document` a `FilePreview` (named by `metadata.name`), `subagent` a `SubagentCard` (`finished` success, `suspended` pending, an `error` of `Stopped` canceled, Stop calls `stop()`), and `activity` parts plus the `activity` messages before a turn one `AgentActivity` that reads `content.title`, `content.status` and `content.detail`. A pending generic interrupt is an `AgentQuestion` after the turn it arrived in (Submit calls `resolveInterrupt(answer)`, Skip `cancel()`), kept with its outcome once it leaves `interrupts`. A tool with `editable` lets the reader edit its JSON arguments and resolves `{ editedArgs }`; `input-streaming` reads `streaming` and a `cancelled` result `canceled`. The blocks take `queue` (shown in a `PromptQueue`; the composer then sends while busy through the new opt-in PromptInput `queueable`, spec v7), `moreMessages` / `loadingMore` / `load-more` for `Conversation`, and `connection` / `resuming` for a Reconnecting status or a Connection lost alert. | AI-SIZE | The size limits move once for the whole AI run, from the merged tree: `llms-full.txt` 2,200,000 → 2,300,000 bytes (measured 2,292,628 after merging LanguageSelect), `catalog.json` 1.36 → 1.385 MB (measured 1,375,768), `catalog/` 1.34 → 1.375 MB (1,364,289), the package unpacked 33.4 → 34.5 MB (33,991,246) and packed 8.05 → 8.26 MB (8,132,209). The growth is text only: eight new components and one block, each with its source, examples, catalog and llms pages; the lanes' separate raises are replaced by these.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | done                                | `packages/webkit/scripts/build-llms.mjs`, `scripts/package-size-budget.json`, `test/catalog/catalog-split.test.mjs`                                                                                                                                                                        |
| AI-GAP  | The chat blocks close the last TanStack AI gaps with no adapter. `attachable` (with `accept`, `maxFiles`, `maxFileSize`) adds `PromptInputAttachButton`, and `send` then hands over TanStack's `MultimodalContent` (`{ content: [text, image/audio/video/document parts with base64 data sources] }`; still a plain string without files), so `@send="(_e, content) => sendMessage(content)"`; user turns draw their media parts. `structured-output` parts draw as a JSON `CodeBlock` (`partial` or `raw` while streaming, `data` when complete, an `Alert` on error). A `tool-result` with text and image content parts draws as text and an `ImageGallery`. `interruptErrors` shows an `Alert` with Retry that emits `retry-interrupts` (`retryInterrupts()`); `unbound` interrupts are asked as an `AgentQuestion` without Skip and emit `answer-interrupt (event, interrupt, value)` for the app to route, since the chat cannot resolve them. `hydrating` (`isHydrating`) shows `Skeleton` rows and disables the composer; `sessionGenerating` shows Responding in another tab. A tool marked `answerable` (with `options`) in `tools` is asked as an `AgentQuestion` until it has an output, and emits `answer-tool (event, { toolCallId, tool, output })` for `addToolResult`. `ai-action-approval` takes the structured output, rich results, interrupt errors and generic or unbound questions, not attachments.    | done                                | `packages/webkit/blocks/ai/{ai-chat,ai-chat-drawer,ai-action-approval}`, `src/test/blocks/ai/ai-chat.test.ts`, `src/test/blocks/ai.test.ts`, `apps/demos/src/pages/ai-chat-page.vue`                                                                                                       |
| RV-AC-1 | Accordion follows the shadcn / Carbon / Geist pattern: every item but the last draws one divider under itself in both states, the trigger draws none, and `Accordion.Content` pads its body on the trigger's inset (after the chevron when it leads) with `--spacing-xs` above and `--spacing-md` below; `padded="false"` keeps edge-to-edge content (a LogViewer, table rows) flush. Answer text is `text-body-sm` at both sizes; the leading gap is `--spacing-xs` as specified, and the chevron reserves a `1em` box. ## 2026-11-20 — Code quality before main

| ID    | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Status      | Where                                                                                                |
| ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| CQ-01 | Before 6.0 merges to main, every component and block is brought to one standard of code quality, maintainability and extension (owner, 2026-11-20, after the Button / Select / Table comparison with shadcn and Cloudscape).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | open        | ROADMAP.md "Plan to launch" 1.7                                                                      |
| CQ-02 | The work follows one general technical spec, not a per-component list: limits per file and function, nothing written twice, extension points on every component, an `options` data prop and one state contract for lists, one announcer, `disabledReason`, native table elements, blocks as folders with variants as states.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | open        | `.claude/docs/CODE_QUALITY.md`                                                                       |
| CQ-03 | The run decides every point itself, comparing shadcn and Cloudscape (our rules first, the stricter reference for accessibility, composition for anatomy and props for data, no size budget raised, then less code), and records each decision as a `CQ-<n>` row here. The owner is asked only for a push.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | open        | `.claude/docs/CODE_QUALITY.md` § 2                                                                   |
| CQ-04 | Pilot on Button and PhoneInput (2026-11-20). A long class string is a `ROOT_CLASS` constant of string literals joined with `+`, one group per line, not a multi-line `class` in the template (the formatter collapses it and its ignore comment is rendered in development). A component named after a native tag (Button) renders its one root through a functional `h(tag)` root, because `component :is="'button'"` resolves to itself.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | implemented | `button.vue`, `phone-input.vue`, `CODE_QUALITY.md` § 3.3                                             |
| CQ-05 | Button announces `loadingLabel` through the new shared `useAnnouncer` when `loading` starts and has no built-in "Loading" text (Cloudscape's `loadingText` works the same way); a default would load the translation runtime, 1.1 KB, into every component that holds a button. PhoneInput announces the number of matching countries or its `noMatch` text; its `searchEmptyLabel` prop is renamed `noMatch`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | implemented | `use-announcer`, `button.vue`, `phone-input.vue`, `v6-props.json`, `CODE_QUALITY.md` § 3.8           |
| CQ-06 | `disabledReason` on Button keeps the disabled button focusable (`aria-disabled`) and puts the reason in `title`, with no Tooltip. Loading the Tooltip from Button, even on demand, added it to the measured size of 97 components and moved 34 KB of FilterMenu to its first load. A sighted keyboard user does not see the native title; an app that needs that wraps the button in `Tooltip`. Button grew 0.35 KB, so 92 budgets that contain it rose by about 0.4 KB each, with a `Budget-raise:` line each.                                                                                                                                                                                | implemented | `button.vue`, `.size-limit.json`, `docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md`, `CODE_QUALITY.md` § 2 and § 3.9 |
| CQ-07 | Landing the pilot on the branch after 251 newer commits: Button keeps the `safeHref` guard, PhoneInput keeps `pointer-coarse:text-body-md` on the input tag itself (the touch-text test reads the tag), 95 size budgets raised by the measured growth (`Budget-raise:` lines in `size-budgets-v6.md`), and the `llms-full.txt` budget goes from 2,345,000 to 2,350,000 bytes for the two new props and the announcer page.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | implemented | `packages/webkit/.size-limit.json`, `packages/webkit/scripts/build-llms.mjs`                         |
| CQ-08 | 2026-11-20: a CPU-only look-alike check (`check:look-alikes`) runs in CI from now on, before the rest of the 1.7 gates. It fails a new pair of exported components that score 35% or more on props, behaviour, classes, template shape and catalog purpose (rare items weighted over common ones) while neither imports the other; the 28 pairs found today are its baseline, which only shrinks. Calibrated by hand on 25 pairs: the Button family, Select and MultiSelect, Input and PasswordInput, ContextMenu and DropdownMenu, DatePicker and DateRangePicker are in; sibling parts that share only a title or a section's standard props stay under the line. Listed as FAM7 in `CI.md`. | implemented | `packages/webkit/scripts/check-look-alikes.mjs`, `.claude/docs/CI.md`                                |

A control is never drawn bigger on a coarse pointer when an invisible hit area can carry the 44 px: fields (Input and so Combobox, Select and MultiSelect triggers, DatePicker and DateRangePicker trigger, clear, split and step buttons), NumberInput steppers, ThemeSwitcher segments and the site header home mark get the Button pattern, an `aria-hidden` `span` with `data-slot="touch-target"` and `absolute hidden inset-[min(0rem,calc(50%-22px))] pointer-coarse:block`; to fit it, Select, MultiSelect, DatePicker and DateRangePicker drop the `overflow-hidden` their frame never needed, and NumberInput stops clipping only on a coarse pointer. Where a hit area would be clipped or would overlap a neighbour, the row grows on a coarse pointer only (`pointer-coarse:min-h-11`): menu rows (Select, MultiSelect, DropdownMenu and ContextMenu, Command), SidebarMenu rows (so the site header's phone navigation), Tabs triggers (the list scrolls and clips), Accordion headers (stacked flush) and FooterLink (the column drops its gap on a coarse pointer). Owner call: menus get a larger hit area on touch, and the 1 px fill inset of RV-N1 stays. ConsoleShowcaseSection keeps the large ToggleGroup at every width and makes it fluid below 40rem instead of shrinking it to small. Kept as decided before: Pagination buttons keep a vertical-only hit area (growing them was rejected in its spec); HighlightButton clips its glow, so it has no hit area yet; SettingsGroup's hover-only copy-link button gets none, since it cannot be seen on touch. | done   | the components named; their specs (Accessibility, Responsive); `packages/webkit/src/test/touch-targets.test.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| -----  | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------                                                                                                                                                                        |

External gets six categories: `ai-tool` (cursor, windsurf, microsoft-copilot, langgraph and mcp move there), `database` (neon, turso, upstash, redis move there), `cms` (wordpress moves there), `observability`, `identity`, `security`; github moves to `developer-tool`.
