## 2026-11-19 — Keyboard focus (audit round 3, lane L04)

| ID   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Status | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| KF01 | Every layout opens with a skip link (owner 2026-11-19: "do it"). `AppLayout` renders a `SkipLink` as its first focusable element, jumping to its content region, so `ConsoleLayout` and every console shell get it by construction; `DocLayout` and `SiteLayout` render one that jumps to their `<main>`. The shell blocks (`shell/shell-console`, `shell/shell-docs`, `shell/shell-site`) get it from their layout, so they render none of their own.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | done   | `app-layout.vue`, `doc-layout.vue`, `site-layout.vue`; the Accessibility sections of `.specs/app-layout.md`, `console-layout.md`, `doc-layout.md`, `site-layout.md`                                                                                                                                             |
| KF02 | A menu holds only menu items: the console account menu's theme choice is a `Theme` group of three radio items (System, Dark, Light) and Upgrade to Pro is a menu item, instead of a `ThemeSwitcher` and a `Button` the arrow keys skip. Tab out of an open menu closes it and moves focus to the next control after its trigger (WAI-ARIA menu button pattern); Shift+Tab returns to the trigger.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | done   | `blocks/shell/shell-console` (and its variants), `apps/demos/src/app.vue`, `use-menu-root.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| L05  | Links and frames never take a script URL (2026-11-19, audit round 3 lane L05). One shared check, `safeHref`, keeps `http`, `https`, `mailto`, `tel`, relative and `#` links and drops `javascript:`, `data:`, `vbscript:`, any other scheme and protocol-relative `//host`, also behind mixed case, spaces, tabs or HTML entities. A dropped link renders as plain text with no link: Button and IconButton stay a native button (a `download` attribute keeps `blob:` / `data:`), Link and BreadcrumbItem point at `#`, Citation is the unlinked note, Markdown-style links in FaqSection, ArticleBodySection and StreamingResponse render their label as text. Embed frames only absolute `http(s)` URLs and always carries a fixed `sandbox` with no top-level navigation; any other `src` shows the title as text. Image, video and audio `src` are not checked (a script URL does not run there).                                      | done   | `packages/webkit/src/utils/safe-href.ts`; the Accessibility section of the specs whose rendering changes (button, icon-button, highlight-button, link, breadcrumb-item, sources, citation, dropdown-menu, faq-section, article-body-section, embed, streaming-response); every other link only loses its `href` |
| PF01 | Performance fixes from the round-3 audit (lane L12, 2026-11-19). The docs build turns off Rolldown's common-chunk merging (`experimental.chunkOptimization.mergeCommonChunks: false`), because it parked the bundler runtime in the lazy Monaco entry and every page preloaded about 1 MB gzip of editor code; initial JS on the docs home falls from 1,378 to 391 KB gzip. AppLayout reads its own width on mount, before the first paint, so a phone never paints the desktop rail (CLS 0.3555 to 0 on the demos routes); the AppLayout Default example now starts with the tools closed, as its spec lists, so it opens no modal drawer on a phone. Docs example previews (component pages and the components index) are not server-rendered: they mount when they come near the viewport, one per idle slot, under `content-visibility: auto` (DataTable page HTML 4.1 to 1.9 MB, TBT on a 4x-throttled phone 8.1 to 1.2 s in the lab). | done   | `apps/docs/vite.config.ts`, `app-layout.vue`, `examples/app-layout/default.vue`, `apps/docs/src/components/example-card.vue`, `live-preview.vue`, `apps/docs/src/lib/mount-queue.ts`                                                                                                                            |

## 2026-11-20 — Keyboard shortcuts built once (keyboard standard, lane K2)

| ID        | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Status  | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| KB-K1     | Screens register shortcuts with one composable, `useShortcuts({ keys, label, group, handler, when, element })` (`@aziontech/webkit/use-shortcuts`); the `?` list is generated from those registrations, never written by hand. `meta` means Command on Apple and Control elsewhere, the same as `Kbd` and `Command`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | done    | `src/composables/use-shortcuts/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| KB-K2     | The registry needs no provider: one page-wide registry with one `keydown` listener (added on the first registration, removed on the last, never on the server). `provideShortcuts()` scopes a separate registry for an app shell, a second Vue root or a test. `ConfigProvider` is not changed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | done    | `use-shortcuts.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| KB-K3     | Guards every shortcut gets: single-key shortcuts (no Command, Control or Alt) never fire while typing in an input, textarea, select, contenteditable or a combobox/textbox/searchbox/spinbutton role, nor with a modifier held; no shortcut fires during IME composition, on auto-repeat, or on an event already default-prevented. Modifier shortcuts (`meta+s`, `meta+enter`) fire inside fields. A punctuation key (`?`, `/`) matches by its character whatever Shift does. The most recent registration wins; the browser default is prevented when a handler runs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | done    | `use-shortcuts.ts`, `use-shortcuts.test.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| KB-K4     | While a modal layer (`aria-modal="true"`) is open, only shortcuts whose `element` sits inside that layer fire, so page shortcuts (`/`, `?`, `meta+s`) are silent behind a dialog or drawer. Escape stays with the overlays (`useOverlayLayer`), not the registry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | done    | `use-shortcuts.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| KB-K5     | `ShortcutsDialog` (`@aziontech/webkit/shortcuts-dialog`) is the one list: `?` opens it, groups render as `role="group"` with an `h3`, rows as `dt`/`dd` with `Kbd`. A shortcut without `label` works but is not listed; one without `handler` is listed only (for keys another part binds, such as `Command`'s `meta+k`). `toAriaKeyshortcuts(keys)` writes `aria-keyshortcuts` for buttons and menu items (`Meta+S Control+S`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | done    | `.specs/shortcuts-dialog.md`, `examples/shortcuts-dialog/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| KB-K6     | `Command` and `SearchTrigger` keep their own `⌘K` binding (unchanged); wiring blocks, layouts and demos to `useShortcuts` / `ShortcutsDialog` is lane K4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | open    | lane K4                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| A3-K3     | Keyboard standard (owner 2026-11-20) checked in CI: `apps/visual/tests/keyboard.spec.ts` presses Tab through the docs home and foundations, the first example of every component, every block primary state and every demo route at 1280, and fails on a control Tab never reaches, a stop with no visible ring, a trap outside a modal, the wrong entry focus, or Esc on a dirty full-page create form that skips the guard. A block is a task when it is in the `create` group or its `block.json` says `"keyboard": { "entry": "field" }`; `"escape": "cancel"` marks the full-page create blocks. Known violations live in `apps/visual/keyboard-baseline.json`, which only shrinks (lane K4).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | done    | `apps/visual/tests/keyboard.ts`, `apps/visual/scripts/keyboard.mjs`, `block.json` `keyboard`                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| A3-L19a   | Owner request 2026-11-20: `LanguageSelect` looks like the `PhoneInput` country picker (supersedes the look in RV-FL-1). Each option is a locale with a country (`en-US`, `pt-BR`, `es-ES`, the new defaults), shown with that country's round flag and the language named in itself; `label` is optional (named by `Intl.DisplayNames` in its own language), and the flag falls back to the tag's likely region (`en` → `US`). New `variant`: `outline` (default) is the full field for forms and settings; `ghost` is the compact header switcher, a round flag and the country code (`US`). The code is the region, not the language, because it matches the flag beside it and tells regional variants apart (`en-US` / `en-GB`); when two options share a country it shows the whole tag (`FR-CA`), with no country the language (`EO`). The rows drop the tag column. Flags are round through `flagStyle(code, sprite, 'round')`; `PhoneInput` keeps its rectangular flags. | done    | `.specs/language-select.md` (v2); `src/components/input/language-select/`; `src/utils/country-flags.ts`; `examples/language-select/`                                                                                                                                                        |

No switch added where none existed (console header, creation shell, sign-in). `ThemeSwitcher` takes the header outline `--border-default` instead of `--border-muted`. The sweep also moved the header `Select`s to `variant="ghost"`, as `Select`'s own spec asks ("never put a bordered field in the header"): the time zone in `view/view-events-explorer` (query) and the Period / Quarter / Time range picker of all 11 dashboard headers. The site footer and site layout budgets grow by the picker (Budget-raise in `docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md`).                                                                     | done    | `apps/docs/src/components/docs-shell.vue`; `apps/demos/src/components/demo-bar.vue`; `.specs/site-footer-section.md`; `.specs/theme-switcher.md`; `blocks/shell/shell-docs/`; `blocks/settings/settings-form/`; `blocks/dashboard/*`; `blocks/view/view-events-explorer/variants/query.vue` |

## 2026-11-20 — Colour logos are paid per logo (audit round 4, lane R6)

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Status | Where it lives                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| R6-01   | `import '@aziontech/icons'` loads the icon font only. The colour logos (`az-color az-<name>`, about 85 KB gzip for the 120) ship as Tailwind v4 utilities in `@aziontech/icons/azionicons-color-utilities`, which `@aziontech/webkit/styles` imports, so an app compiles only the logos its sources or its WebKit files name. The whole set stays at `@aziontech/icons/azionicons-color` for apps without Tailwind and pages that show every logo; the docs icons page loads it lazily. The `css-icons` fixture in `.size-limit-css.json` budgets the icons CSS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | done   | `packages/icons/scripts/build-color-css.mjs`, `packages/webkit/src/styles.css`, `packages/webkit/.size-limit-css.json`, `MIGRATION_V6.md`                                                                                                                                                   |
| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Status | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| KB-K1   | Screens register shortcuts with one composable, `useShortcuts({ keys, label, group, handler, when, element })` (`@aziontech/webkit/use-shortcuts`); the `?` list is generated from those registrations, never written by hand. `meta` means Command on Apple and Control elsewhere, the same as `Kbd` and `Command`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | done   | `src/composables/use-shortcuts/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| KB-K2   | The registry needs no provider: one page-wide registry with one `keydown` listener (added on the first registration, removed on the last, never on the server). `provideShortcuts()` scopes a separate registry for an app shell, a second Vue root or a test. `ConfigProvider` is not changed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | done   | `use-shortcuts.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| KB-K3   | Guards every shortcut gets: single-key shortcuts (no Command, Control or Alt) never fire while typing in an input, textarea, select, contenteditable or a combobox/textbox/searchbox/spinbutton role, nor with a modifier held; no shortcut fires during IME composition, on auto-repeat, or on an event already default-prevented. Modifier shortcuts (`meta+s`, `meta+enter`) fire inside fields. A punctuation key (`?`, `/`) matches by its character whatever Shift does. The most recent registration wins; the browser default is prevented when a handler runs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | done   | `use-shortcuts.ts`, `use-shortcuts.test.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| KB-K4   | While a modal layer (`aria-modal="true"`) is open, only shortcuts whose `element` sits inside that layer fire, so page shortcuts (`/`, `?`, `meta+s`) are silent behind a dialog or drawer. Escape stays with the overlays (`useOverlayLayer`), not the registry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | done   | `use-shortcuts.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| KB-K5   | `ShortcutsDialog` (`@aziontech/webkit/shortcuts-dialog`) is the one list: `?` opens it, groups render as `role="group"` with an `h3`, rows as `dt`/`dd` with `Kbd`. A shortcut without `label` works but is not listed; one without `handler` is listed only (for keys another part binds, such as `Command`'s `meta+k`). `toAriaKeyshortcuts(keys)` writes `aria-keyshortcuts` for buttons and menu items (`Meta+S Control+S`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | done   | `.specs/shortcuts-dialog.md`, `examples/shortcuts-dialog/`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 2026-11-20 — Quality rules start with an empty list

| ID        | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Where it lives                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| CQ-E      | Owner, 2026-11-20: until 6.0 merges into `main` everything counts as new, so every new quality rule starts with an **empty list** (no baseline of known violations). The rule is kept as written and the code is fixed. Applies first to line length (200 characters, class strings grouped into script constants per `CODE_QUALITY.md` 3.3), then to copy-pasted code and to the full accessibility lint. A line that is one unbreakable token (a path, one class) is exempt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done for all three, with no baseline and no inline disable (`noInlineConfig`). Line length and the full `eslint-plugin-vuejs-accessibility` set (every rule that is not deprecated) run in `lint:quality` (gate `quality-lint`); copy-pasted code runs in `check:duplication`, where repeats inside components or inside one block now share a composable or helper. The accessibility lint reads native elements only (a WebKit component carries its own semantics and is linted in its own file); a list role on `ul`/`ol` is allowed (it restores list semantics that `list-style: none` removes in Safari); captions are required on `video`, not on `audio` (AudioPlayer shows its transcript). Repeats between two blocks follow CQ-E2. The 142 budget raises it cost are in `size-budgets-v6.md`. | `packages/webkit/eslint.quality.config.js`, `packages/webkit/scripts/check-duplication.mjs`, `packages/webkit/scripts/run-gates.mjs`, `.github/workflows/governance.yml`, `packages/webkit/docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md` |
| CQ-E2     | Owner, 2026-11-21: repeats between two blocks follow shadcn. A shadcn block is self-contained and is copied into an app on its own, so two blocks may repeat their own sample data and their own markup (sidebar-01 to sidebar-16 each carry their nav data). Logic that several blocks need is a registry item (`registry:lib`, `registry:hook`) that each block imports, and Vercel's AI Elements take the AI SDK message types instead of redefining them. In WebKit the shared types and helpers move into the package and each block imports them; `sample.ts` and the `.vue` files stay the block's own.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done. `check:duplication` fails a repeat between two blocks when both sides are logic modules (`.ts` other than `sample.ts`), and reports the rest (sample data and markup). The TanStack AI message types and helpers that `ai-chat`, `ai-chat-drawer` and `ai-action-approval` each copied are now `@aziontech/webkit/utils/ai-chat`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `packages/webkit/scripts/check-duplication.mjs`, `packages/webkit/src/utils/ai-chat.ts`, `.claude/docs/CODE_QUALITY.md`                                                                                                     |
| CQ-E3     | Owner, 2026-11-21: keyboard resizing of DataTable columns is not needed. The resize handle stays mouse and touch only and is hidden from screen readers (`aria-hidden`); column width is not a task a keyboard user has to do.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done, no spec change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `table-head-cell.vue`, `.specs/data-table.md`                                                                                                                                                                               |
| CQ-E4     | Owner, 2026-11-21: fix every look-alike pair, no baseline. A pair is reuse when one component imports the other **or both import the same private part, `lib/` module or composable** (used by at most eight components, so shared infrastructure such as `useTranslate` does not count): that is option 2 of `CODE_QUALITY.md` 3.3, which the check could not see. Fixed by sharing: Button, IconButton and HighlightButton render one root (`useButtonRoot`); Input, NumberInput and PasswordInput one frame (`input/parts/input-frame.ts`) and `useFieldAttrs().controlState`; ContextMenu and DropdownMenu one panel; DropdownMenu.Trigger and Popover.Trigger one trigger part; DateRangePicker DatePicker's control, trigger and popover parts; ChartArea and ChartLine one series layer, and DonutChart and MapChart Chart's frame, data table, legend button and tooltip panel; six page sections one frame (`FeatureTabsSectionShell`); the index sections one facet select and toolbar; the media-split sections one media part. Select and MultiSelect, SubagentCard and ToolCall, ConsoleLayout and DocLayout already shared their pieces. One pair is accepted with its reason: `article-cards-section::investor-marks-section` (article cards and an investor logo wall). | Done. `known` is empty.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `packages/webkit/scripts/lib/look-alikes.mjs`, `scripts/look-alikes-baseline.json`, `test/standards/look-alikes.test.mjs`; the parts named in the decision                                                                  |
| CQ-E5     | Owner, 2026-11-21: empty the other lists (authoring outside i18n, rule and skill documents, component completeness, block data). CodeBlock lines switch motion through `data-motion` / `data-enter-from` instead of a class map; the unused Tabs map is gone; TransferList and PanelBody keep their classes inline or in grouped constants; Skeleton uses `animate-shimmer`; the zero-unit checks (token engine and the theme build) accept `0rem` in a `var()` fallback inside `calc()`; the spec animation check reads the private parts a component imports from another component; Select's spec rows name its animations. The three skills find components through the MCP. AppLayout and HeroFormSection gain the examples their specs list, IconButton an icon-transition example, and `password-requirements-root` is exported. The three shell blocks take their records through typed props with `sample.ts` fallbacks; ShellSite gains a default slot.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Done. `authoring-docs-baseline.json`, `complete-baseline.json` and `blocks-data-baseline.json` are empty; `authoring-baseline.json` holds only the hard-coded site strings (roadmap 1.9).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | the files named in the decision, `.claude/hooks/lib/spec-compliance-checks.mjs`, `packages/theme/scripts/lib/zero-unit.mjs`                                                                                                 |
| CQ-E6     | Owner, 2026-11-21: the 249 hard-coded interface strings of the marketing site (236 in the `ConsoleMockup` widgets) move into the message catalogs later, as roadmap 1.9, so the site can be translated; until then they are the only list of known violations. Fixes that change what a person sees or hears, found while sharing code: NumberInput and PasswordInput take Input's readonly look, ring gap, touch target and click-to-focus, and NumberInput's adornments are hidden from screen readers; DatePicker leaves an Escape an inner control handled; DonutChart's legend key keeps its colour under forced colours; ToolCall's chevron animates (`transition-[rotate]`); FeatureWidgetsSection's secondary action animates its chevron and renders no empty actions row; the media-split sections pass `hovered` to ConsoleMockup, so the console reacts to hovering the band; the DropdownMenu panel carries `role="presentation"` as ContextMenu's does.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Done                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `ROADMAP.md` 1.9; the components named                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| CQ-E7     | Owner, 2026-11-21 (CI from an empty list): `as never` and `as unknown as` are zero in `src`, blocks and examples, and the count of other type assertions only falls. The one form kept is `undefined as never` as the default of an unbound `defineModel` (27 open and value models): Vue 3.5 offers no other way to keep an unbound model `undefined` while typing its update event as the value type (the no-default form widens every `update:*` event to `T \| undefined` for apps).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Done. `check:assertions` accepts exactly that form inside a `defineModel` options object.                                                     | `packages/webkit/scripts/check-assertions.mjs`, `test/standards/assertions.test.mjs`                                                                                                                                        |
| CQ-E8     | Owner, 2026-11-21: the size limits of `CODE_QUALITY.md` 3.1 hold with no exemption, even when splitting a function or a root costs gzip bytes. Each budget the split grows is raised to its measured size with a `Budget-raise:` line naming the split and the base size; the lanes first cut the cost (plain arguments instead of option and return objects, fewer call boundaries). Largest costs: DateRangePicker +1.8 KB, AttributeEditor and TagEditor +1.6 KB, SchemaForm +2.1 KB, Trace +1.4 KB, DataGrid +1.7 KB, PropertyFilter +1.8 KB; about +0.1 KB on every overlay from `usePlacement`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Done                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `packages/webkit/docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md`, `.size-limit.json`                                                                                                                                                       |
| CQ-E9     | Owner, 2026-11-21: every native control element, ARIA widget role and the spin and pulse marker classes have an owner; a file outside the owner writes none (CI.md COPY1–5). Owners are listed in `owners.json`, each with its reason; blocks and examples own nothing. New API so that no exception list is needed: `Link` `inline` (a link inside a sentence), `useFilePicker`, `AppHeaderAccount`, `Card` and `BentoGrid.Cell` `href` (the whole surface is one link), `Footer` `homeHref` / `homeAriaLabel`, the catalogue animation `animate-status-pulse`, the icons `az-arrow-right-to-line` and `az-arrow-left-to-line` (TransferList, which now mirrors in right-to-left). | LS-TAG    | Owner, 2026-11-22: the compact `LanguageSelect` shows the language, named by the international standard, not the country (reverses the code rule of A3-L19a). The short code is the option's BCP 47 tag (RFC 5646: ISO 639 language, ISO 3166-1 region) in capitals, exactly as the option names it: `EN`, `PT-BR`, `EN-GB`, `EN-CA`. The main variant of a language is the bare language and a regional variant adds its region, so the built-in options are `en`, `pt-BR` and `es`, the same tags as the shipped catalogs (they were `en-US`, `pt-BR`, `es-ES`). A regional variant shares its language's catalog or has its own: `ConfigProvider` looks up the tag, then the tag cut from the right (`en-GB` → `en`), then the language. | CQ-E      | Owner, 2026-11-20: until 6.0 merges into `main` everything counts as new, so every new quality rule starts with an **empty list** (no baseline of known violations). The rule is kept as written and the code is fixed. Applies first to line length (200 characters, class strings grouped into script constants per `CODE_QUALITY.md` 3.3), then to copy-pasted code and to the full accessibility lint. A line that is one unbreakable token (a path, one class) is exempt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done for all three, with no baseline and no inline disable (`noInlineConfig`). Line length and the full `eslint-plugin-vuejs-accessibility` set (every rule that is not deprecated) run in `lint:quality` (gate `quality-lint`); copy-pasted code runs in `check:duplication`, where repeats inside components or inside one block now share a composable or helper. The accessibility lint reads native elements only (a WebKit component carries its own semantics and is linted in its own file); a list role on `ul`/`ol` is allowed (it restores list semantics that `list-style: none` removes in Safari); captions are required on `video`, not on `audio` (AudioPlayer shows its transcript). Repeats between two blocks follow CQ-E2. The 142 budget raises it cost are in `size-budgets-v6.md`. | `packages/webkit/eslint.quality.config.js`, `packages/webkit/scripts/check-duplication.mjs`, `packages/webkit/scripts/run-gates.mjs`, `.github/workflows/governance.yml`, `packages/webkit/docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md` |
| CQ-E2     | Owner, 2026-11-21: repeats between two blocks follow shadcn. A shadcn block is self-contained and is copied into an app on its own, so two blocks may repeat their own sample data and their own markup (sidebar-01 to sidebar-16 each carry their nav data). Logic that several blocks need is a registry item (`registry:lib`, `registry:hook`) that each block imports, and Vercel's AI Elements take the AI SDK message types instead of redefining them. In WebKit the shared types and helpers move into the package and each block imports them; `sample.ts` and the `.vue` files stay the block's own.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done. `check:duplication` fails a repeat between two blocks when both sides are logic modules (`.ts` other than `sample.ts`), and reports the rest (sample data and markup). The TanStack AI message types and helpers that `ai-chat`, `ai-chat-drawer` and `ai-action-approval` each copied are now `@aziontech/webkit/utils/ai-chat`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `packages/webkit/scripts/check-duplication.mjs`, `packages/webkit/src/utils/ai-chat.ts`, `.claude/docs/CODE_QUALITY.md`                                                                                                     |
| CQ-E3     | Owner, 2026-11-21: keyboard resizing of DataTable columns is not needed. The resize handle stays mouse and touch only and is hidden from screen readers (`aria-hidden`); column width is not a task a keyboard user has to do.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done, no spec change.                                                                                                                                                                |
| CQ-E4     | Owner, 2026-11-21: fix every look-alike pair, no baseline. A pair is reuse when one component imports the other **or both import the same private part, `lib/` module or composable** (used by at most eight components, so shared infrastructure such as `useTranslate` does not count): that is option 2 of `CODE_QUALITY.md` 3.3, which the check could not see. Fixed by sharing: Button, IconButton and HighlightButton render one root (`useButtonRoot`); Input, NumberInput and PasswordInput one frame (`input/parts/input-frame.ts`) and `useFieldAttrs().controlState`; ContextMenu and DropdownMenu one panel; DropdownMenu.Trigger and Popover.Trigger one trigger part; DateRangePicker DatePicker's control, trigger and popover parts; ChartArea and ChartLine one series layer, and DonutChart and MapChart Chart's frame, data table, legend button and tooltip panel; six page sections one frame (`FeatureTabsSectionShell`); the index sections one facet select and toolbar; the media-split sections one media part. Select and MultiSelect, SubagentCard and ToolCall, ConsoleLayout and DocLayout already shared their pieces. One pair is accepted with its reason: `article-cards-section::investor-marks-section` (article cards and an investor logo wall). | Done. `known` is empty.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `packages/webkit/scripts/lib/look-alikes.mjs`, `scripts/look-alikes-baseline.json`, `test/standards/look-alikes.test.mjs`; the parts named in the decision                                                                  |
| CQ-E5     | Owner, 2026-11-21: empty the other lists (authoring outside i18n, rule and skill documents, component completeness, block data). CodeBlock lines switch motion through `data-motion` / `data-enter-from` instead of a class map; the unused Tabs map is gone; TransferList and PanelBody keep their classes inline or in grouped constants; Skeleton uses `animate-shimmer`; the zero-unit checks (token engine and the theme build) accept `0rem` in a `var()` fallback inside `calc()`; the spec animation check reads the private parts a component imports from another component; Select's spec rows name its animations. The three skills find components through the MCP. AppLayout and HeroFormSection gain the examples their specs list, IconButton an icon-transition example, and `password-requirements-root` is exported. The three shell blocks take their records through typed props with `sample.ts` fallbacks; ShellSite gains a default slot.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Done. `authoring-docs-baseline.json`, `complete-baseline.json` and `blocks-data-baseline.json` are empty; `authoring-baseline.json` holds only the hard-coded site strings (roadmap 1.9).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | the files named in the decision, `.claude/hooks/lib/spec-compliance-checks.mjs`, `packages/theme/scripts/lib/zero-unit.mjs`                                                                                                 |
| CQ-E6     | Owner, 2026-11-21: the 249 hard-coded interface strings of the marketing site (236 in the `ConsoleMockup` widgets) move into the message catalogs later, as roadmap 1.9, so the site can be translated; until then they are the only list of known violations. Fixes that change what a person sees or hears, found while sharing code: NumberInput and PasswordInput take Input's readonly look, ring gap, touch target and click-to-focus, and NumberInput's adornments are hidden from screen readers; DatePicker leaves an Escape an inner control handled; DonutChart's legend key keeps its colour under forced colours; ToolCall's chevron animates (`transition-[rotate]`); FeatureWidgetsSection's secondary action animates its chevron and renders no empty actions row; the media-split sections pass `hovered` to ConsoleMockup, so the console reacts to hovering the band; the DropdownMenu panel carries `role="presentation"` as ContextMenu's does.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Done                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `ROADMAP.md` 1.9; the components named                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| CQ-E7     | Owner, 2026-11-21 (CI from an empty list): `as never` and `as unknown as` are zero in `src`, blocks and examples, and the count of other type assertions only falls. The one form kept is `undefined as never` as the default of an unbound `defineModel` (27 open and value models): Vue 3.5 offers no other way to keep an unbound model `undefined` while typing its update event as the value type (the no-default form widens every `update:*` event to `T \| undefined` for apps).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Done. `check:assertions` accepts exactly that form inside a `defineModel` options object.                                                                          |
| CQ-E8     | Owner, 2026-11-21: the size limits of `CODE_QUALITY.md` 3.1 hold with no exemption, even when splitting a function or a root costs gzip bytes. Each budget the split grows is raised to its measured size with a `Budget-raise:` line naming the split and the base size; the lanes first cut the cost (plain arguments instead of option and return objects, fewer call boundaries). Largest costs: DateRangePicker +1.8 KB, AttributeEditor and TagEditor +1.6 KB, SchemaForm +2.1 KB, Trace +1.4 KB, DataGrid +1.7 KB, PropertyFilter +1.8 KB; about +0.1 KB on every overlay from `usePlacement`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Done                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `packages/webkit/docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md`, `.size-limit.json`                                                                                                                                                       |
| CQ-E9     | Owner, 2026-11-21: every native control element, ARIA widget role and the spin and pulse marker classes have an owner; a file outside the owner writes none (CI.md COPY1–5). Owners are listed in `owners.json`, each with its reason; blocks and examples own nothing. New API so that no exception list is needed: `Link` `inline` (a link inside a sentence), `useFilePicker`, `AppHeaderAccount`, `Card` and `BentoGrid.Cell` `href` (the whole surface is one link), `Footer` `homeHref` / `homeAriaLabel`, the catalogue animation `animate-status-pulse`, the icons `az-arrow-right-to-line` and `az-arrow-left-to-line` (TransferList, which now mirrors in right-to-left). Sidebar drives its collapse from the panel's `data-state` instead of a hidden checkbox. CopyPromptButton's glow turns once and stops (WCAG 2.2.2). Chip, FlowDiagram links, the DateRangePicker quick ranges, SuggestionGroup and AvatarGroup own their element with a reason. | LS-TAG    | Owner, 2026-11-22: the compact `LanguageSelect` shows the language, named by the international standard, not the country (reverses the code rule of A3-L19a). The short code is the option's BCP 47 tag (RFC 5646: ISO 639 language, ISO 3166-1 region) in capitals, exactly as the option names it: `EN`, `PT-BR`, `EN-GB`, `EN-CA`. The main variant of a language is the bare language and a regional variant adds its region, so the built-in options are `en`, `pt-BR` and `es`, the same tags as the shipped catalogs (they were `en-US`, `pt-BR`, `es-ES`). A regional variant shares its language's catalog or has its own: `ConfigProvider` looks up the tag, then the tag cut from the right (`en-GB` → `en`), then the language. The flag still comes from `country`, the tag's region or its likely region (`en` → `US`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | done (owner request, 2026-11-22)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `.specs/language-select.md`; `src/components/input/language-select/`; `src/composables/use-locale/use-locale.ts`; `examples/language-select/`; `rules/webkit-strings.md`                                                    |

## 2026-11-22 — The AI chat at ChatGPT quality, in the Azion brand
