## 2026-11-22 — The logo orange is never a text colour

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Status | Where it lives                                                                                                                            |
| ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| BRAND-1 | Owner, 2026-11-22: no accessibility exceptions for brand colour. The logo orange `#F3652B` stays the main colour (`--primary`) and is never the colour of text, which missed WCAG AA on light surfaces (about 3:1). Brand parts mark default text instead: the Eyebrow and Quote name and comparison group titles take an orange bar, LinkDirectory column titles and a hovered linked FeatureItem an orange underline, the HeroTitle highlight and `Tag variant="highlight"` an orange fill with black text (6.7:1); the FieldLabel asterisk and Stats prefixes use the text colour. `--text-brand` is removed in 6.0 (it was never released; `--text-primary` now migrates to `--text-default`), `a11y-accepted.json` is empty, and `off-brand-orange` blocks both orange text tokens. Supersedes the brand-text exceptions of 2026-11-16 (Eyebrow) and 2026-11-18 (every brand text). | Done   | `.claude/rules/styling.md` § One orange, `rules/webkit-tokens.md`, the ten component specs, `packages/webkit/src/test/a11y-accepted.json` |

## 2026-11-22 — No accepted look-alike

| ID   | Decision   | Status | Where it lives                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| LA-1 | Owner, 2026-11-22: the last accepted look-alike (ArticleCardsSection and InvestorMarksSection, 40%) is fixed by sharing code, not accepted. Both render the private `SectionBand` frame (section, Module, ModuleHeader, RegistrationFrame) and `linkedCell` (safe link, `noopener noreferrer` on a new tab, one `--bg-hover` fill and inset focus ring), so every linked cell hovers and focuses the same way. `look-alikes-baseline.json` is empty. The other ~31 sections move onto `SectionBand` in roadmap 1.7.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Done   | `packages/webkit/src/components/section/parts/section-band.vue`, `section/lib/linked-cell.ts`                                                                                                                                 |
| LA-2 | 2026-11-23, roadmap 1.7 (owner asked for the move with "each page as we have them today can still be built the same way"): the page sections draw their outer frame through the private `SectionBand` part, which now also takes the band's accessible name (`label`), header size, padding and description width, an unframed body, and `header`, `header-actions` and `after` slots. `SectionHatch` draws the hatched closing strip and `SectionActions` the header buttons (kept out of `SectionBand` so a band without buttons does not bundle `Button`). `FeatureTabsSectionShell` is removed. FeatureTabsSection and FeatureScrollSection lost their family link (the shell) and share their floating code window instead (`SectionCodeWindow`; FeatureScrollSection still loads it lazily), so `look-alikes-baseline.json` stays empty. 35 of 46 sections use it. Left out, because they are a different kind of band: `hero-section`, `hero-form-section`, `site-header-section`, `site-footer-section`, `copy-command-section` (not a framed band), `pricing-plans-section` (its own side-bordered frame, no module), `card-carousel-section` (a carousel, no module), `process-scroll-section` (a pinned scroll runway), `article-body-section` (an article page inside a `Container`), and `faq-section` and `closing-call-to-action-section`, whose tests pin a plain `div` root so the band keeps one landmark (the closing band uses `SectionHatch`). Rule kept: no public API change, tests unchanged, pixel- and accessibility-tree-identical (952 before/after captures of every section example, light, dark, 390 px, right-to-left). Linked cells keep their current hover; `linkedCell` is applied in the visual review (1.5). Each section measured alone grows 0.1 to 0.35 KB (it carries the part); all 46 together shrink 1,615.95 → 1,615.56 KB. | Done   | `packages/webkit/src/components/section/parts/section-band.vue`, `parts/section-hatch.vue`, `parts/section-actions.vue`, `parts/section-code-window.vue`, `section/lib/section-actions.ts`, `docs/reviews/size-budgets-v6.md` |

## 2026-11-23 — Required fields: the asterisk alone

| ID    | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Status | Where it lives                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| REQ-1 | Owner, 2026-11-23: a required field is marked by a lone `*` after its label (`Field.Label`, `SettingsRow`); the "(Required)" word is gone and optional fields stay unmarked. The `*` is `aria-hidden`; screen readers hear "required" from the control (`required` / `aria-required`), or, for a control whose role cannot take it (DatePicker, DateRangePicker, FileDropzone, TagEditor), from the Field's hidden "Required" text in its `aria-describedby`. Marking optional fields instead (the Cloudscape and GOV.UK way) was compared and kept for later. Validation stays in the app: one `errors` object per form, each `Field` reads its entry. | done   | `field-label.vue`, `field.vue`, `use-field-control.ts`, `src/test/field-required.test.ts`, `.specs/field.md`, `skills/webkit-form/SKILL.md` § Required and optional |

The `//` prefix marks every marketing section header (`module-header`, `call-to-action`, `media-split`, `plan-comparison-section`, `hero-form-section`, `hero-section` by default); `<>` / `</>` are the exception, only for a band about code itself; app and docs eyebrows take no prefix. ## 2026-11-23 — Azion Engineering Playbook compliance

| ID    | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Status      | Where                                                                                                                                               |

| CMP-2 | The security scan of `azion-action-security` runs inline on a GitHub-hosted runner, because that action is private and runs on azion-runners, which a public repository cannot use: Gitleaks over the full history, osv-scanner over the lockfiles and Semgrep (community ERROR rules), same versions and flags, on every pull request to `main`, on `main` and weekly, job name `Security Scan`. The 40 Gitleaks findings in history are sample values except two in the removed sample site (a blog post API key, an Azion origin-key), listed for Security Office in `.gitleaksignore`. | implemented | `.github/workflows/security.yml`, `.gitleaksignore`                                                                                                 |
| CMP-3 | Every release packs the tarball once, writes an SBOM of what a consumer installs (Syft, SPDX and CycloneDX), stops on a high or critical vulnerability (Grype), signs the tarball (Cosign keyless) and publishes that exact tarball with provenance; the SBOM, signature and `SHA256SUMS` go on the GitHub Release. The four `publish-*.yml` keep their names (npm trusted publishing checks the calling workflow) and call one `release-package.yml`.                                                                                                                                     | implemented | `.github/workflows/release-package.yml`, `publish-*.yml`, `publish-workflows.test.mjs`                                                              |
| CMP-4 | Owner, 2026-11-23: the four packages share one version and release together with one `vX.Y.Z` tag, one GitHub Release and one root `CHANGELOG.md` (release-please root package with `extra-files` for the four `package.json`); theme and icons jump to 6.0.0 with WebKit. The changelog check now passes with no waiver. The tags made before the switch stay untouched (owner), so the tag check keeps one dated waiver (2027-05-31) that covers only them. `last-release-sha` points at the 5.13.0 release commit until `v6.0.0` ships, then it is deleted.                             | implemented | `release-please-config.json`, `.release-please-manifest.json`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `release-please.yml`, `release-package.yml`, `.azion-compliance.yml` |
| CMP-5 | Changes to `.github/`, `CODEOWNERS`, `MAINTAINERS.md` and the security tool files need `@aziontech/oss-review` (the company rule for public repositories); `SECURITY.md`, `security.yml`, `release-package.yml` and the scanner exception files also need `@aziontech/security-office`. Vulnerability reports go to security@azion.com.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | implemented | `CODEOWNERS`, `SECURITY.md`                                                                                                                         |
